# Round 0: start

Saved as `R0.md` (start set).

## Examined
- **Seeds:** all 18 references in `0-start/seeds.md` (rows R0-001 to R0-018), resolved to OpenAlex records. Ungar (1987) has no OpenAlex record of the book; its row uses the UC Berkeley EECS report page found by web search. Myers (1982) resolves only to the 1978 first-edition record.
- **References named in the SQs:** the 17 papers the protocol names as evidence in SQ9 to SQ19 (rows R0-019 to R0-035), resolved by OpenAlex title lookup. Kept at gate 2b (decision D4 = a).
- **Search string, first pass:** the 18 blocks B1 to B14 in `search-string.md`, run on OpenAlex on 2026-10-06; the first 12 hits by relevance and the first 12 by citations of each block were pre-screened (rows R0-036 to R0-383).
- **Search string, rerun after gate 2b (2026-10-06):** all results of blocks B1k, B3, B4, B5 and B14 (decision D2 = c), and the first 12 + 12 hits of the new block B15 (decision D7 = b; 766 results, above the 300 limit for a full screen). New rows R0-384 to R0-1108.
- **Reference named in `sls/1-protocol/need.md`:** Ditzel and Patterson (1980), pre-screened against I2 (row R0-1109), and a two-page 1998 note on it found by the same lookup (row R0-1110).
- **Changed criterion:** I3 as changed on 2026-10-06 (`sls/1-protocol/changes/2026-10-06-i3-shared-memory-baseline.md`) was applied to every bug-study row. R0-005 (Lu et al. 2008) changes from exclude X1 to include I3. No other existing row changes: the other bug-study rows are either already I3 includes (R0-009, and the actor and Go studies) or detection, testing or verification tools that report no empirical defect study, which the change does not affect. The new B3 rows were screened against the changed I3.

Pre-screening used title and abstract (OpenAlex abstract, or the publisher or report page where OpenAlex had none). A row that says "full text decides" is a proposed include because title and abstract could not settle it. *Source* names where the candidate was found: `seeds.md`, an SQ file, `need.md`, or the OpenAlex block(s) that returned it; "(full screen)" marks the blocks whose results were all screened. Records of the same work (reprints, proceedings and journal versions, repeated deposits) are merged into one row and listed under "Other records"; merges were made where the normalised title and first author match, and by hand where the titles differ (listed in the row). No earlier round exists, so no candidate is marked as seen. Rows R0-001 to R0-383 keep their numbers from the first pass. *Decision* and *P ID* are left empty for gate 2b.

Totals: 1110 rows; 275 proposed include (I1: 159, I2: 9, I3: 4, I4: 103; 130 of them say "full text decides"), 835 proposed exclude (X1: 678, of which 421 are hits from other fields or unrelated computing topics; X2: 20, X3: 21, X5: 75, X6: 15, X8: 2, X9: 24).
